Sort so that the last record in each group is the latest by time, with row order as tie-break.\n        # NaT sorts last by default for ascending=False? To be explicit, sort ascending and take tail(1)\n        # after sorting by (__event_time, __row_order).\n        df = df.sort_values([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\", \"__event_time\", \"__row_order\"],\n                            ascending=[True, True, True, True, True])\n    else:\n        df = df.sort_values([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\", \"__row_order\"],\n                            ascending=[True, True, True, True])\n\n    latest = df.groupby([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\"], as_index=False).tail(1)\n\n    lookup: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], float] = {}\n    for _, r in latest.iterrows():\n        key = (str(r[\"Facility\"]).strip(), str(r[\"Session ID\"]).strip(), str(r[\"Bin ID\"]).strip())\n        lookup[key] = float(r[\"Count Qty\"])  # concrete numeric\n\n    return lookup\n\n\ndef _compute_formatted_df(plan_df: pd.DataFrame, final_lookup: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], float]) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    # Validate plan columns\n    missing = [c for c in REQUIRED_PLAN_COLS if c not in plan_df.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Cycle_Plan missing required columns: {missing}. Variance\"].iat[i]\n\n        # Default to 0 if missing count.\n        ag = 0\n        if mfc == 0 and approval_needed == \"YES\":\n            # Be defensive about numeric types.\n            exp = float(exp_qty) if not pd.isna(exp_qty) else float(\"nan\")\n            allowed = float(allowed_var) if not pd.isna(allowed_var) else float(\"nan\")\n            diff = abs(exp - float(count_qty))\n Final Count\"\n        elif mfc == 0 and ag == 1:\n            es = \"Approval Gap\"\n        else:\n            es = \"Missing Final Count, Approval Gap\"\n\n        missing_final.append(int(mfc))\n _build_final_count_lookup(events_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], float]:\n    \"\"\"Map (Facility, Session ID, Bin ID) -> Count Qty for latest FINAL event.\"\"\"\n\n    required_cols = Preserve column order as provided in file.\n    return df\n\n\ndef _build_final_count_lookup(events_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], float]:\n    \"\"\"Map (Facility, Session ID, Bin ID) -> Count Qty for latest FINAL event.\"\"\"\n\n    required_cols = {\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\", \"Event Type\", \"Count Qty\"}\n    missing = required_cols - set(events_df.columns)\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Count_Event_Log missing required columns: {sorted(missing)}\")\n\n    df = events_df.copy()\n\n    # Normalize keys as strings (strip) to avoid mismatches.\n    for col in [\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\"]:\n        df[col] = df[col].astype(str).str.strip()\n\n    # 3) Keep only the LAST per (Facility, Session ID, Bin ID)\n    df[\"__row_order\"] = range(len(df))\n\n    if \"Event Time\" in df.columns:\n        # Parse if possible; if unparseable, treat as NaT and fall back to row order.\n        parsed = pd.to_datetime(df[\"Event Time\"], errors=\"coerce\", utc=False)\n        df[\"__event_time\"] = parsed\n        # Sort so that the last record in each group is the latest by time, with row order as tie-break.\n        # NaT sorts last by default for ascending=False? To be explicit, sort ascending and take tail(1)\n        # after sorting by (__event_time, __row_order).\n        df = df.sort_values([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\", \"__event_time\", \"__row_order\"],\n                            ascending=[True, True, True, True, True])\n    else:\n        df = df.sort_values([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\", \"__row_order\"],\n                            ascending=[True, True, True, True])\n\n    latest = df.groupby([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\", \"Bin ID\"], as_index=False).tail(1)\n\n    lookup: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], float] = {}\n    for _, r in latest.iterrows():\n        key = (str(r[\"Facility\"]).strip(), str(r[\"Session ID\"]).strip(), str(r[\"Bin ID\"]).strip())\n        lookup[key] = float(r[\"Count Qty\"])  # concrete numeric\n\n    return lookup\n\n\ndef _compute_formatted_df(plan_df: pd.DataFrame, final_lookup: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], float]) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    # Validate plan columns\n    missing = [c for c in REQUIRED_PLAN_COLS if c not in plan_df.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Cycle_Plan missing required columns: {missing}.
